letting v be 8
letting b be 28
letting r be 14
letting rv be product([r,v;int(1..)])
letting rvmodb be rv % b
letting floorrv be (rv / b)
letting ceilrv be sum([(rv / b),toInt(rv % b != 0);int(1..)])
letting num be (sum([product([product([ceilrv,ceilrv;int(1..)]),rvmodb;int(1..)]),product([product([floorrv,floorrv;int(1..)]),(b - rvmodb);int(1..)]);int(1..)]) - rv)
letting denom be product([v,(v - 1);int(1..)])
letting lb_lambda be sum([(num / denom),toInt(num % denom != 0);int(1..)])
